class="flex-1"><h1 class="mb-5 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-center gap-2 text-center text-lg sm:justify-start md:text-left md:text-2xl"><span>Radio Mega Haiti</span><!----></h1><div class="hidden-scroll overflow-x-scroll mb-5"><div class="flex gap-2 md:flex-wrap"><!--[--><a href="/radio/genres/News/Talk/" class="tag-link inline-flex h-9 items-center gap-1 rounded-full border border-zeno-gray-border-light px-5 whitespace-nowrap transition duration-150 hover:border-zeno-dark pointer-coarse:h-11"><!--[--><span class="iconify i-solar:global-linear shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" style="font-size:18px;"></span><span class="text-sm sm:text-base"><!--[--> English (U.S.)<!--]--></span><!----><!--]--></a><!--]--></div></div><div class="description wrap-break-word text-zeno-gray mt-5"><p>Radio Mega Haiti is a Haitian news and talk radio station available online through Zeno.FM, serving listeners with trusted information, cultural connection, and engaging programming in <strong>Haitian Creole, French, and English</strong>. Known as <strong>“Le plus grand réseau radio haïtien,”</strong> the station has built a strong identity as a Haitian-owned and operated radio broadcaster with more than a decade of service to the Haitian community. </p><p>Through its Zeno.FM page, Radio Mega Haiti gives listeners easy access to live broadcasts from anywhere in the world. Whether tuning in from Haiti, South Florida, the Bahamas, or the Haitian diaspora abroad, audiences can stay connected to news, conversations, music, and issues that matter to their daily lives. The station is listed under the <strong>News/Talk</strong> category and offers programming that reflects the voice, concerns, and culture of Haitian listeners.</p><p>Radio Mega Haiti stands out for its focus on high-quality journalism and community-centered broadcasting. Its programming reaches a large Haitian audience, especially in South Florida, one of the largest Haitian communities in the United States. The station also reaches listeners in the Bahamas and the northwest region of Haiti, helping strengthen the connection between Haitians at home and abroad.</p><p>In addition to news and talk programming, Radio Mega Haiti includes the sound and spirit of Caribbean culture, with rhythms such as Konpa and other familiar musical styles. The station’s Zeno.FM page also features podcasts such as <strong>Boukante la Parole</strong>, <strong>Mega Booster Live</strong>, <strong>Mega Fun</strong>, <strong>Mega Matin</strong>, <strong>Pour OU contre</strong>, and <strong>Son Lari a</strong>, giving listeners more ways to enjoy its content.</p><p>For anyone looking for Haitian radio with information, culture, community, and Caribbean energy, Radio Mega Haiti is a strong choice.</p><p><strong>Radio Mega Haiti — connecting Haitian voices across the world.</strong></p></div><div class="hidden-scroll overflow-x-scroll mt-5"><div
